  <script>
    let board             = null;
    let boardSize         = 9;
    let playerColor       = 'B';
    let claudeColor       = 'W';
    let difficulty        = 'match';
    let moveNumber        = 0;
    let sgfMoves          = [];
    let gameOver          = false;
    let consecutivePasses = 0;
    let userId            = null;
    let userProfile       = null;
    let savedGameId       = null;
    let teachingPauseFired = false;
    let teachingPauseSuppressed = false;
    let commentaryGen     = 0; // incremented each move; prevents stale async commentary from overwriting newer text
    let engineRetryPending = false; // true while showing "engine offline — retry" UI
    let engineRetrySnapshot = null;
    let handicapStones    = []; // GTP coordinate strings for Black's pre-placed handicap stones
    let humanSLEnabled    = false; // true when katago-service has the human SL model loaded

    async function init() {
      const session = await Auth.getSession();
      if (!session) { location.href = 'auth.html'; return; }
      userId      = session.user.id;
      userProfile = await UserDB.getProfile(userId);
      // Check once whether the human SL model is available. When it is, we skip
      // handicap stones because humanSL already plays at the player's rank level.
      try {
        const r = await fetch('/.netlify/functions/engine-status');
        if (r.ok) { const d = await r.json(); humanSLEnabled = !!d.humanSL; }
      } catch (_) { /* non-fatal — fall back to visit-throttling + handicap */ }
    }

    function selectSize(el) {
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-size]').forEach(e => e.classList.remove('selected'));
      el.classList.add('selected');
      boardSize = parseInt(el.dataset.size);
    }
    function selectColor(el) {
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-color]').forEach(e => e.classList.remove('selected'));
      el.classList.add('selected');
      playerColor = el.dataset.color;
      claudeColor = playerColor === 'B' ? 'W' : 'B';
    }
    function selectDiff(el) {
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-diff]').forEach(e => e.classList.remove('selected'));
      el.classList.add('selected');
      difficulty = el.dataset.diff;
    }

    async function startGame() {
      document.getElementById('setup-screen').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('game-screen').style.display  = 'block';

      // Compute handicap from effective rank (so difficulty adjusts it too).
      // Skip when humanSL is active — the engine already plays at the player's rank level.
      const handicapCount = humanSLEnabled ? 0 : rankToHandicap(getEffectiveRank());
      handicapStones = (HANDICAP_POSITIONS[boardSize] ?? [])[Math.min(handicapCount, 9)] ?? [];
      if (handicapStones.length > 0) {
        playerColor = 'B';
        claudeColor = 'W';
      }

      const pStone = document.getElementById('tag-player-stone');
      const cStone = document.getElementById('tag-claude-stone');
      pStone.className = `player-tag__stone player-tag__stone--${playerColor.toLowerCase()}`;
      cStone.className = `player-tag__stone player-tag__stone--${claudeColor.toLowerCase()}`;
      document.getElementById('commentary-color').textContent = playerColor === 'B' ? 'Black' : 'White';

      board = Board.create(document.getElementById('board-container'), boardSize, onPlayerMove);

      // Place handicap stones on the board before the game begins.
      if (handicapStones.length > 0) {
        for (const gtp of handicapStones) {
          const col = GTPCOLS.indexOf(gtp[0]);
          const row = boardSize - parseInt(gtp.slice(1)); // 0-indexed from top
          board.place(col, row, 'B');
        }
        setCommentary(`You have ${handicapStones.length} handicap stone${handicapStones.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}. Goxira plays first.`);
      }

      board.setColor('B');
      updateTurnIndicator('B');

      // White plays first in handicap games (Black's stones are already placed).
      if (playerColor === 'W' || handicapStones.length > 0) await claudeMove();
    }

    // ── Legal move helpers ────────────────────────────────────────
    function getNeighbors(col, row) {
      return [[col-1,row],[col+1,row],[col,row-1],[col,row+1]]
        .filter(([c,r]) => c >= 0 && c < boardSize && r >= 0 && r < boardSize);
    }

    function getGroup(col, row, stones) {
      const color = stones[`${col},${row}`];
      if (!color) return [];
      const group = [], visited = new Set(), queue = [[col, row]];
      while (queue.length) {
        const [c, r] = queue.pop();
        const key = `${c},${r}`;
        if (visited.has(key)) continue;
        visited.add(key);
        if (stones[key] !== color) continue;
        group.push([c, r]);
        getNeighbors(c, r).forEach(([nc, nr]) => { if (!visited.has(`${nc},${nr}`)) queue.push([nc, nr]); });
      }
      return group;
    }

    function getLiberties(group, stones) {
      const libs = new Set();
      group.forEach(([c, r]) => {
        getNeighbors(c, r).forEach(([nc, nr]) => { if (!stones[`${nc},${nr}`]) libs.add(`${nc},${nr}`); });
      });
      return libs.size;
    }

    function isLegalMove(col, row, color, stones) {
      if (stones[`${col},${row}`]) return false;
      const opponent   = color === 'B' ? 'W' : 'B';
      const testStones = { ...stones, [`${col},${row}`]: color };
      // Legal if captures opponent
      for (const [nc, nr] of getNeighbors(col, row)) {
        if (testStones[`${nc},${nr}`] === opponent) {
          if (getLiberties(getGroup(nc, nr, testStones), testStones) === 0) return true;
        }
      }
      // Legal if own group has liberties
      return getLiberties(getGroup(col, row, testStones), testStones) > 0;
    }

    function hasLegalMoves(color) {
      const stones = board.getStones();
      for (let c = 0; c < boardSize; c++)
        for (let r = 0; r < boardSize; r++)
          if (isLegalMove(c, r, color, stones)) return true;
      return false;
    }

    // Pick a random legal move for the given color (used as last-resort fallback)
    function getRandomLegalMove(color, stones) {
      const legal = [];
      for (let c = 0; c < boardSize; c++)
        for (let r = 0; r < boardSize; r++)
          if (isLegalMove(c, r, color, stones)) legal.push({ col: c, row: r });
      if (!legal.length) return null;
      return legal[Math.floor(Math.random() * legal.length)];
    }

    function isBoardEffectivelyOver() {
      const stones = board.getStones();
      const filled = Object.keys(stones).length;
      if (filled / (boardSize * boardSize) >= 0.85) return true;
      if (!hasLegalMoves(playerColor) && !hasLegalMoves(claudeColor)) return true;
      return false;
    }

    // ── Player move ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    async function onPlayerMove(col, row) {
      if (gameOver) return;

      const snapshot = { stones: board.getStones(), sgfLength: sgfMoves.length, moveNumber };

      const result = board.place(col, row, playerColor);
      if (result.illegal) {
        // Suicide or occupied — board.place() already removed the stone visually.
        // Do NOT add to SGF or advance the game state; just let the player try again.
        setCommentary('That intersection is not legal — the stone would have no liberties. Try somewhere else.');
        board.setInteractive(true);
        board.setColor(playerColor);
        return;
      }

      consecutivePasses = 0;
      moveNumber++;
      addSGFMove(playerColor, col, row);
      updateMoveCount();
      updateTurnIndicator(claudeColor);
      commentaryGen++;
      setCommentary('…');
      board.setInteractive(false);
      document.getElementById('undo-btn').disabled = true;

      await claudeMove(snapshot);
    }

    // ── Goxira move ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    async function claudeMove(snapshot = null) {
      if (gameOver) return;

      // No legal moves — auto pass
      if (!hasLegalMoves(claudeColor)) {
        consecutivePasses++;
        addSGFPass(claudeColor);
        setCommentary('Goxira has no legal moves — passing.');
        if (consecutivePasses >= 2 || isBoardEffectivelyOver()) { endGame('double-pass'); return; }
        updateTurnIndicator(playerColor);
        board.setColor(playerColor);
        board.setInteractive(true);
        if (snapshot) enableUndo(snapshot);
        return;
      }

      document.getElementById('claude-thinking').classList.add('show');

      try {
        // KataGo is the only engine. If it is unavailable, we stop cleanly
        // rather than substituting Claude or a random move — both corrupt the game record.
        const movePayload = JSON.stringify({
          sgf: buildSGF(), color: claudeColor, boardSize,
          rank: getEffectiveRank(), currentStones: board.getStones(),
          ...(handicapStones.length > 0 ? { handicapStones, komi: 0.5 } : {}),
        });
        const res = await fetch('/.netlify/functions/katago-move', {
          method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: movePayload,
        }).then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : null).catch(() => null);

        if (!res?.move) {
          document.getElementById('claude-thinking').classList.remove('show');
          board.setInteractive(false);
          if (engineRetryPending) {
            // Second failure — give up, save and stop cleanly.
            engineRetryPending = false;
            engineRetrySnapshot = null;
            document.getElementById('retry-engine-btn').style.display = 'none';
            gameOver = true;
            setCommentary(
              "Goxira's engine is offline and couldn't be reached. " +
              'Your game has been saved — use "New" to start a fresh game, ' +
              'or tap Review Game to look at what you played.'
            );
            try {
              const session = await Auth.getSession();
              if (session) {
                const saved = await UserDB.saveGame(session.user.id, buildSGF(), boardSize, 'live_play');
                savedGameId = saved?.id ?? null;
              }
            } catch(saveErr) { console.error('Save game on engine-down error:', saveErr); }
            document.getElementById('game-over-card').classList.add('show');
          } else {
            // First failure — offer retry without ending the game.
            engineRetryPending = true;
            engineRetrySnapshot = snapshot;
            setCommentary("Goxira's engine didn't respond. Tap the button below to try again — your game is still going.");
            document.getElementById('retry-engine-btn').style.display = '';
          }
          return;
        }

        // Engine responded — clear any retry state.
        if (engineRetryPending) {
          engineRetryPending = false;
          engineRetrySnapshot = null;
          document.getElementById('retry-engine-btn').style.display = 'none';
        }

        const move = res.move;
        document.getElementById('claude-thinking').classList.remove('show');

        if (move.col === -1 && move.row === -1) {
          consecutivePasses++;
          addSGFPass(claudeColor);
          // Teaching pause: first deliberate pass triggers the modal
          if (shouldTeachingPause()) {
            setCommentary('Goxira would like to pause and reflect on the game.');
            showTeachingPause(snapshot);
            return;
          }
          setCommentary(`Goxira passes. ${move.thinking || ''}`);
          if (consecutivePasses >= 2 || isBoardEffectivelyOver()) { endGame('double-pass'); return; }
        } else {
          if (!isLegalMove(move.col, move.row, claudeColor, board.getStones())) {
            // KataGo returned an illegal move — this should not happen in normal operation.
            // Do not substitute a random move. Pass and log for investigation.
            console.error('KataGo returned illegal move', move, 'at turn', moveNumber);
            consecutivePasses++;
            addSGFPass(claudeColor);
            setCommentary('Goxira passes.');
            if (consecutivePasses >= 2 || isBoardEffectivelyOver()) { endGame('double-pass'); return; }
          } else {
            consecutivePasses = 0;
            board.place(move.col, move.row, claudeColor);
            moveNumber++;
            addSGFMove(claudeColor, move.col, move.row);
            updateMoveCount();
            setCommentary('Goxira played. Claude is analysing…');
            // Fetch teaching commentary asynchronously — don't block the game.
            // Pass res.analysis (already fetched above) so analyze-move skips its own KataGo call.
            const gen = ++commentaryGen;
            const gtpMove = coordsToGTP(move.col, move.row);
            API.analyzeMove(
              buildSGF(), moveNumber, boardSize,
              userProfile?.current_rank || '20 kyu',
              gtpMove, claudeColor, board.getStones(),
              res || null
            ).then(r => { if (commentaryGen === gen && r?.message) setCommentary(r.message); })
             .catch(() => {});
          }
        }

        if (isBoardEffectivelyOver()) { endGame('double-pass'); return; }

        updateTurnIndicator(playerColor);
        board.setColor(playerColor);
        board.setInteractive(true);
        if (snapshot) enableUndo(snapshot);

      } catch(e) {
        console.error('Goxira move error:', e);
        document.getElementById('claude-thinking').classList.remove('show');
        board.setInteractive(false);
        if (engineRetryPending) {
          // Already retried once — give up.
          engineRetryPending = false;
          engineRetrySnapshot = null;
          document.getElementById('retry-engine-btn').style.display = 'none';
          gameOver = true;
          setCommentary(
            'Goxira ran into an error. Your game has been saved — ' +
            'tap "Review Game" to see what you played, or "New" to start fresh.'
          );
          try {
            const session = await Auth.getSession();
            if (session) {
              const saved = await UserDB.saveGame(session.user.id, buildSGF(), boardSize, 'live_play');
              savedGameId = saved?.id ?? null;
            }
          } catch(saveErr) { console.error('Save game on error:', saveErr); }
          document.getElementById('game-over-card').classList.add('show');
        } else {
          engineRetryPending = true;
          engineRetrySnapshot = snapshot;
          setCommentary("Goxira ran into a network error. Tap the button below to try again.");
          document.getElementById('retry-engine-btn').style.display = '';
        }
      }
    }

    // ── Engine retry ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    async function retryEngineMove() {
      if (!engineRetryPending) return;
      document.getElementById('retry-engine-btn').style.display = 'none';
      setCommentary('Trying again…');
      const snap = engineRetrySnapshot;
      await claudeMove(snap);
    }

    // ── Undo ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function enableUndo(snapshot) {
      const btn = document.getElementById('undo-btn');
      btn.disabled = false;
      btn._snapshot = snapshot;
    }

    function undoMove() {
      const btn  = document.getElementById('undo-btn');
      const snap = btn._snapshot;
      if (!snap || gameOver) return;
      board.setState(snap.stones);
      board.setColor(playerColor);
      sgfMoves   = sgfMoves.slice(0, snap.sgfLength);
      moveNumber = snap.moveNumber;
      consecutivePasses = 0;
      updateMoveCount();
      updateTurnIndicator(playerColor);
      board.setInteractive(true);
      btn.disabled  = true;
      btn._snapshot = null;
      setCommentary('Move taken back. Your turn.');
    }

    // ── Pass / Resign ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    async function passTurn() {
      if (gameOver) return;
      consecutivePasses++;
      addSGFPass(playerColor);
      moveNumber++;
      updateMoveCount();
      setCommentary('You passed.');
      document.getElementById('undo-btn').disabled = true;
      if (consecutivePasses >= 2 || isBoardEffectivelyOver()) { endGame('double-pass'); return; }
      board.setInteractive(false);
      updateTurnIndicator(claudeColor);
      await claudeMove();
    }

    function resignGame() {
      if (gameOver) return;
      endGame('resign');
    }

    // ── Area scoring (Chinese rules) ────────────────────────────
    function areaScore() {
      const stones = board.getStones();
      const komi = handicapStones.length > 0 ? 0.5 : (boardSize === 19 ? 7.5 : boardSize === 13 ? 6.5 : 5.5);
      let blackStones = 0, whiteStones = 0;

      // Count stones on the board
      for (const key in stones) {
        if (stones[key] === 'B') blackStones++;
        else if (stones[key] === 'W') whiteStones++;
      }

      // Flood-fill to find territories
      const visited = new Set();
      let blackTerritory = 0, whiteTerritory = 0;

      for (let r = 0; r < boardSize; r++) {
        for (let c = 0; c < boardSize; c++) {
          const key = `${c},${r}`;
          if (visited.has(key) || stones[key]) continue;

          // BFS to find connected empty region and its bordering colors
          const region = [];
          const queue = [[c, r]];
          const borders = new Set();
          visited.add(key);

          while (queue.length) {
            const [qc, qr] = queue.shift();
            region.push([qc, qr]);
            for (const [nc, nr] of [[qc-1,qr],[qc+1,qr],[qc,qr-1],[qc,qr+1]]) {
              if (nc < 0 || nc >= boardSize || nr < 0 || nr >= boardSize) continue;
              const nk = `${nc},${nr}`;
              if (stones[nk]) { borders.add(stones[nk]); continue; }
              if (visited.has(nk)) continue;
              visited.add(nk);
              queue.push([nc, nr]);
            }
          }

          // Territory belongs to a color only if bordered by exactly one color
          if (borders.size === 1) {
            if (borders.has('B')) blackTerritory += region.length;
            else whiteTerritory += region.length;
          }
        }
      }

      const blackTotal = blackStones + blackTerritory;
      const whiteTotal = whiteStones + whiteTerritory + komi;
      return { blackTotal, whiteTotal, komi };
    }

    function scoreMessage(score) {
      const diff = Math.abs(score.blackTotal - score.whiteTotal);
      const winner = score.blackTotal > score.whiteTotal ? 'Black' : 'White';
      const playerWins = (playerColor === 'B' && winner === 'Black')
                      || (playerColor === 'W' && winner === 'White');
      const who = playerWins ? 'You win' : 'Goxira wins';
      return `${who} — ${winner} by ${diff.toFixed(1)} points (area scoring, ${score.komi} komi).`;
    }

    // ── Teaching pause ──────────────────────────────────────────
    function getRankTier() {
      const rank  = userProfile?.current_rank || '20 kyu';
      const match = rank.match(/(\d+)\s*(kyu|dan)/);
      if (!match) return 'beginner';
      if (match[2] === 'dan') return 'advanced';
      const kyu = parseInt(match[1]);
      if (kyu >= 16) return 'beginner';
      if (kyu >= 7) return 'intermediate';
      return 'advanced';
    }

    function shouldTeachingPause() {
      if (teachingPauseFired || teachingPauseSuppressed || gameOver) return false;
      if (moveNumber < 10) return false; // too early to pause
      return true;
    }

    function showTeachingPause(snapshot) {
      teachingPauseFired = true;
      const tier = getRankTier();
      const overlay = document.getElementById('teaching-pause');
      const msgEl   = document.getElementById('tp-message');
      const noteEl  = document.getElementById('tp-note');
      const actEl   = document.getElementById('tp-actions');

      // Rank-adaptive messaging
      if (tier === 'beginner') {
        msgEl.textContent = "I think the big fights are settled. This is a great time to practice reading the board — knowing when the game shifts from fighting to counting is one of the hardest skills in Go.";
        noteEl.style.display = 'block';
        noteEl.textContent   = "Let\u2019s keep playing so you can practice endgame moves \u2014 every point matters! I\u2019ll keep coaching you through it.";
        actEl.innerHTML = `
          <button class="btn--primary-tp" onclick="teachingPauseContinue()">Keep playing \u2014 practice the endgame</button>
          <button class="btn--secondary-tp" onclick="teachingPauseReview()">Skip to game review</button>`;
      } else if (tier === 'intermediate') {
        msgEl.textContent = "The borders look mostly decided. Want to play out the endgame, or jump to the review?";
        noteEl.style.display = 'block';
        noteEl.textContent   = "At your level, recognizing when to transition to endgame play is an important skill. The remaining moves are mostly about efficiently claiming the last few points.";
        actEl.innerHTML = `
          <div style="display:flex;gap:10px">
            <button class="btn--equal-tp" style="background:white;color:var(--ink);border:1.5px solid var(--paper-deep)" onclick="teachingPauseContinue()">Play endgame</button>
            <button class="btn--equal-tp" style="background:var(--ink);color:var(--paper);border:none;box-shadow:var(--shadow-md)" onclick="teachingPauseReview()">Jump to review</button>
          </div>`;
      } else {
        msgEl.textContent = "The borders look mostly decided. Want to continue, or jump to the review?";
        noteEl.style.display = 'none';
        actEl.innerHTML = `
          <div style="display:flex;gap:10px">
            <button class="btn--equal-tp" style="background:white;color:var(--ink);border:1.5px solid var(--paper-deep)" onclick="teachingPauseContinue()">Play endgame</button>
            <button class="btn--equal-tp" style="background:var(--ink);color:var(--paper);border:none;box-shadow:var(--shadow-md)" onclick="teachingPauseReview()">Jump to review</button>
          </div>
          <button class="btn--dismiss" onclick="teachingPauseDismiss()">Don\u2019t show these pauses anymore</button>`;
      }

      // Store snapshot so we can resume if player continues
      overlay._snapshot = snapshot;
      overlay.classList.add('show');
    }

    function teachingPauseContinue() {
      const overlay  = document.getElementById('teaching-pause');
      const snapshot = overlay._snapshot;
      overlay.classList.remove('show');
      // The pass was already recorded — just hand turn back to player
      updateTurnIndicator(playerColor);
      board.setColor(playerColor);
      board.setInteractive(true);
      if (snapshot) enableUndo(snapshot);
    }

    function teachingPauseReview() {
      document.getElementById('teaching-pause').classList.remove('show');
      endGame('teaching-pause');
    }

    function teachingPauseDismiss() {
      teachingPauseSuppressed = true;
      teachingPauseContinue();
    }

    // ── End game ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    async function endGame(reason) {
      gameOver = true;
      board.setInteractive(false);
      document.getElementById('claude-thinking').classList.remove('show');
      document.getElementById('undo-btn').disabled = true;

      const title = reason === 'resign' ? 'You resigned'
        : reason === 'teaching-pause' ? 'Game review' : 'Game complete';
      let msg = reason === 'resign'
        ? 'Good game — review it to find where things went wrong.'
        : reason === 'teaching-pause'
        ? 'Good game! Let\u2019s see how it played out.'
        : 'Both players passed. Calculating score…';

      document.getElementById('game-over-title').textContent = title;
      document.getElementById('game-over-msg').textContent   = msg;
      document.getElementById('game-over-card').classList.add('show');

      // Score completed games (not resigns)
      if (reason !== 'resign') {
        // Try KataGo first; fall back to client-side area scoring
        fetch('/.netlify/functions/katago-move', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ sgf: buildSGF(), color: playerColor, boardSize, rank: '1 dan' }),
        }).then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : null).then(data => {
          if (data?.scoreLead != null) {
            const lead = data.scoreLead;
            const ahead = (playerColor === 'B' ? lead > 0 : lead < 0);
            const pts   = Math.abs(lead).toFixed(1);
            const who   = ahead ? 'You lead' : 'Goxira leads';
            document.getElementById('game-over-msg').textContent =
              `${who} by ~${pts} points (KataGo estimate). Review to see how it played out.`;
          } else {
            document.getElementById('game-over-msg').textContent = scoreMessage(areaScore());
          }
        }).catch(() => {
          document.getElementById('game-over-msg').textContent = scoreMessage(areaScore());
        });
      }

      try {
        const saved = await UserDB.saveGame(userId, buildSGF(), boardSize, 'live_play');
        savedGameId = saved.id;
      } catch(e) { console.error('Save game error:', e); }
    }

    function reviewGame() {
      location.href = savedGameId ? `review.html?id=${savedGameId}` : 'review.html';
    }
    function newGame() { location.reload(); }

    // ── SGF ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const SGFCOLS    = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrs';
    const GTPCOLS    = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNOPQRST'; // GTP skips I
    function coordsToGTP(col, row) { return GTPCOLS[col] + (boardSize - row); }
    function addSGFMove(color, col, row) { sgfMoves.push(`;${color}[${SGFCOLS[col]}${SGFCOLS[row]}]`); }
    function addSGFPass(color)           { sgfMoves.push(`;${color}[]`); }
    function buildSGF() {
      // row=0 is top in board coords, which already matches SGF row order, so SGFCOLS[row] is correct.
      const haTag = handicapStones.length > 0
        ? `HA[${handicapStones.length}]KM[0.5]AB` + handicapStones.map(gtp => {
            const col = GTPCOLS.indexOf(gtp[0]);
            const row = boardSize - parseInt(gtp.slice(1)); // 0-indexed from top
            return `[${SGFCOLS[col]}${SGFCOLS[row]}]`;
          }).join('')
        : '';
      return `(;GM[1]FF[4]SZ[${boardSize}]${haTag}${sgfMoves.join('')})`;
    }

    // ── Handicap ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // Standard star-point positions in GTP notation: corners first, sides next, centre last.
    // These counts are estimates calibrated to KataGo's residual strength at each visit level —
    // empirical tuning is recommended once real game data is available.
    const HANDICAP_POSITIONS = {
      9: [
        [],
        ['E5'],
        ['G7','C3'],
        ['G7','C3','C7'],
        ['G7','C3','G3','C7'],
        ['G7','C3','G3','C7','E5'],
        ['G7','C3','G3','C7','E3','E7'],
        ['G7','C3','G3','C7','E3','E7','E5'],
        ['G7','C3','G3','C7','C5','G5','E3','E7'],
        ['G7','C3','G3','C7','C5','G5','E3','E7','E5'],
      ],
      13: [
        [],
        ['G7'],
        ['K10','D4'],
        ['K10','D4','K4'],
        ['K10','D4','K4','D10'],
        ['K10','D4','K4','D10','G7'],
        ['K10','D4','K4','D10','D7','K7'],
        ['K10','D4','K4','D10','D7','K7','G7'],
        ['K10','D4','K4','D10','D7','K7','G4','G10'],
        ['K10','D4','K4','D10','D7','K7','G4','G10','G7'],
      ],
      19: [
        [],
        ['K10'],
        ['Q16','D4'],
        ['Q16','D4','Q4'],
        ['Q16','D4','Q4','D16'],
        ['Q16','D4','Q4','D16','K10'],
        ['Q16','D4','Q4','D16','D10','Q10'],
        ['Q16','D4','Q4','D16','D10','Q10','K10'],
        ['Q16','D4','Q4','D16','D10','Q10','K4','K16'],
        ['Q16','D4','Q4','D16','D10','Q10','K4','K16','K10'],
      ],
    };

    function rankToHandicap(rank) {
      if (!rank) return 0;
      const m = rank.match(/(\d+)\s*(kyu|dan)/i);
      if (!m || m[2].toLowerCase() === 'dan') return 0;
      const kyu = parseInt(m[1]);
      if (kyu >= 20) return 9;
      if (kyu >= 18) return 8;
      if (kyu >= 15) return 6;
      if (kyu >= 12) return 4;
      if (kyu >= 8)  return 3;
      if (kyu >= 5)  return 2;
      return 1; // 1–4 kyu
    }

    // ── UI ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function setCommentary(text) {
      const el = document.getElementById('move-commentary');
      el.textContent = text;
      Glossary.wrap(el);
      el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });
    }

    function updateTurnIndicator(color) {
      const isPlayer = color === playerColor;
      document.getElementById('tag-player').classList.toggle('active',  isPlayer);
      document.getElementById('tag-claude').classList.toggle('active', !isPlayer);
    }
    function updateMoveCount() {
      document.getElementById('move-count').textContent = `Move ${moveNumber}`;
    }
    function getEffectiveRank() {
      const rank  = userProfile?.current_rank || '20 kyu';
      const match = rank.match(/(\d+)\s*(kyu|dan)/);
      if (!match || match[2] !== 'kyu') return rank;
      if (difficulty === 'easier') return `${Math.min(30, parseInt(match[1]) + 5)} kyu`;
      if (difficulty === 'harder') return `${Math.max(1,  parseInt(match[1]) - 5)} kyu`;
      return rank;
    }

    async function getGameHint() {
      if (gameOver) return;
      const btn = document.getElementById('hint-btn');
      btn.disabled    = true;
      btn.textContent = 'Goxira is thinking…';
      try {
        const res = await API.getHint(
          buildSGF(), boardSize,
          userProfile?.current_rank || '20 kyu',
          playerColor, board.getStones(), moveNumber,
        );
        setCommentary('💬 ' + res.commentary);
      } catch(e) {
        setCommentary('Could not get commentary right now.');
      }
      btn.disabled    = false;
      btn.textContent = '💬 Ask Goxira for commentary';
    }

    init();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

        <span class="attempts-label">Attempts:</span>
        <div class="attempt-pip" id="pip-1"></div>
        <div class="attempt-pip" id="pip-2"></div>
        <div class="attempt-pip" id="pip-3"></div>
      </div>

      <!-- Commentary -->
      <div class="commentary-box" id="commentary"></div>

      <!-- Solution -->
      <div class="solution-box" id="solution-box">
        <strong>Solution</strong>
        <span id="solution-text"></span>
      </div>

      <!-- Actions -->
      <div class="action-row">
        <button class="btn btn--secondary" id="hint-btn" onclick="getHint()">💡 Hint</button>
        <button class="btn btn--primary"   id="next-btn" onclick="loadNewProblem()"
                style="display:none">Next Problem →</button>
      </div>

    </div>
  </main>

  <!-- Bottom nav -->
  <nav class="bottom-nav">
    <div class="bottom-nav__item" onclick="location.href='home.html'">
      <span class="bottom-nav__icon">⊞</span>Home
    </div>
    <div class="bottom-nav__item active" onclick="location.href='problems.html'">
      <span class="bottom-nav__icon">🧩</span>Problems
    </div>
    <div class="bottom-nav__item" onclick="location.href='play.html'">
      <span class="bottom-nav__icon">⚫</span>Play

  <!-- Scripts -->
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@supabase/supabase-js@2.49.4/dist/umd/supabase.min.js"></script>
  <script>
    window.GOTUTOR_CONFIG = {
      supabaseUrl:     'https://humvrkmlvhzwoawoyslu.supabase.co',
      supabaseAnonKey: 'sb_publishable_dEQVVToEQh35MPrBEtKkmQ_om0ty8-r',
    };
  </script>
  <script src="supabase-client.js"></script>
  <script src="api.js"></script>
  <script src="board.js"></script>
  <script src="glossary.js"></script>
  <script>
    let currentProblem    = null;
    let board             = null;
    let attemptNumber     = 0;
    let hintsUsed         = 0;
    let solved            = false;
    let isMoving          = false;
    let userId            = null;
    let userProfile       = null;
    let totalSolved       = 0;
    let selectedCategory  = '';   // '' = all, 'life_death', 'tesuji', 'shape'
    let prefetchedProblem = null;  // next problem preloaded in background
    let prefetchPromise   = null;  // in-flight prefetch Promise

    const CATEGORY_DEFS = {
      '':           'Tactical puzzles from all categories — find the vital move to solve each position.',
      'life_death': 'Life & Death: determine whether a group can survive or be killed. Mastering this is the foundation of Go — groups with no two eyes cannot live.',
      'tesuji':     'Tesuji: a clever tactical move that solves a local problem efficiently — often unexpected, exploiting a weakness in the opponent\'s shape.',
      'shape':      'Shape: recognize good and bad stone formations. Efficient shapes strengthen your groups; bad shapes like the empty triangle waste stones.',
    };

    function setCategoryDef(cat) {
      const el = document.getElementById('category-def');
      if (el) el.textContent = CATEGORY_DEFS[cat] || '';
    }

    function selectCategory(btn, cat) {
      selectedCategory = cat;
      document.querySelectorAll('.category-tab').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
      btn.classList.add('active');
      setCategoryDef(cat);
      prefetchedProblem = null;
      prefetchPromise   = null;
      loadNewProblem();
    }


    // ── Init ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    async function init() {
      const session = await Auth.getSession();
      if (!session) { location.href = 'auth.html'; return; }
      userId = session.user.id;
      userProfile = await UserDB.getProfile(userId);
      if (!userProfile) { location.href = 'auth.html'; return; }

      document.getElementById('stat-rank').textContent  = userProfile.current_rank;
      document.getElementById('stat-score').textContent = userProfile.rank_score;
      setCategoryDef(selectedCategory);

      totalSolved = await UserDB.getSolvedCount(userId);
      document.getElementById('stat-solved').textContent = totalSolved;

      // Warm up the KataGo connection and surface engine status to the user.
      // Fire-and-forget: doesn't block problem loading.
      fetch('/.netlify/functions/engine-status')
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(d => {
          if (!d.ok) document.getElementById('engine-badge').classList.add('show');
        })
        .catch(() => {});

      await loadNewProblem();
    }

    // ── Problem prefetch ─────────────────────────────────────────
    // Fires a background request for the next problem so it's ready immediately
    // when the student presses Next/Skip. Called after rendering each problem.
    function schedulePrefetch() {
      if (!userProfile) return;
      prefetchedProblem = null;
      prefetchPromise = API.generateProblem(
          userProfile.current_rank, null, 9, selectedCategory || null, userId
        )
        .then(res => { prefetchedProblem = res.problem; return res.problem; })
        .catch(() => null);
    }

    // ── Load a new problem ───────────────────────────────────────
    async function loadNewProblem() {
      attemptNumber = 0; hintsUsed = 0; solved = false; isMoving = false;
      currentProblem = null;

      document.getElementById('commentary').className   = 'commentary-box';
      document.getElementById('solution-box').className = 'solution-box';
      document.getElementById('next-btn').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('hint-btn').style.display = 'inline-flex';
      document.getElementById('skip-btn').style.display = 'inline-flex';
      document.getElementById('board-loading').style.display = 'flex';
      document.getElementById('topic-badge').textContent = 'Loading…';
      document.getElementById('to-play').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('problem-description').textContent = 'Loading problem…';
      resetPips();

      if (board) {
        const container = document.getElementById('board-container');
        const old = container.querySelector('svg');
        if (old) old.remove();
        board = null;
      }

      try {
        let problem = null;
        if (prefetchedProblem) {
          // Prefetch already resolved — instant handoff.
          problem = prefetchedProblem;
          prefetchedProblem = null;
          prefetchPromise   = null;
        } else if (prefetchPromise) {
          // Prefetch in flight — await it instead of firing a duplicate request.
          problem = await prefetchPromise;
          prefetchedProblem = null;
          prefetchPromise   = null;
        } else {
          // First load (no prefetch yet) or prefetch failed — fetch directly.
          const res = await API.generateProblem(
            userProfile.current_rank, null, 9, selectedCategory || null, userId
          );
          problem = res.problem;
        }

        if (!problem) throw new Error('No problem returned');
        currentProblem = problem;
        renderProblem(currentProblem);
        // Immediately start loading the one after this.
        schedulePrefetch();
      } catch(e) {
        console.error('Problem load error:', e);
        document.getElementById('problem-description').textContent =
          'Failed to load problem. Tap Skip to try again.';
        document.getElementById('board-loading').style.display = 'none';
        // Retry prefetch so Skip works.
        schedulePrefetch();
      }
    }

    // ── Render problem on board ──────────────────────────────────
    function renderProblem(problem) {
      const size      = problem.boardSize || 19;
      const container = document.getElementById('board-container');

      board = Board.create(container, size, onMovePlayed);

      const stones = problem.setup?.stones || {};
      Object.entries(stones).forEach(([key, color]) => {
        const [c, r] = key.split(',').map(Number);
        board.placeSetup(c, r, color);
      });

      const toPlay = problem.setup?.toPlay || 'B';
      board.setColor(toPlay);

      document.getElementById('board-loading').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('topic-badge').textContent = problem.topic || 'problem';
      document.getElementById('problem-description').textContent = problem.description;

      const stone = document.getElementById('to-play-stone');
      stone.className = `to-play__stone to-play__stone--${toPlay.toLowerCase()}`;
      document.getElementById('to-play-label').textContent =
        `${toPlay === 'B' ? 'Black' : 'White'} to play`;
      document.getElementById('to-play').style.display = 'flex';

      // ── Auto-crop board to stone region ──
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        cropViewBox(problem, size);
      });
    }

    // ── Crop SVG viewBox to the active stone region ──────────────
    // Board.js owns all touch/mouse interaction; this only adjusts the
    // visible area so problems render zoomed in on the relevant section.
    function cropViewBox(problem, boardSize) {
      const svg = document.querySelector('#board-container svg');
      if (!svg) return;

      const PADDING = 28;
      const CELL    = 36;
      const total   = PADDING * 2 + CELL * (boardSize - 1);

      const stoneKeys = Object.keys(problem.setup?.stones || {});
      if (!stoneKeys.length) return;

      // Collect all relevant grid coordinates (stones + solution move)
      const allCols = stoneKeys.map(k => parseInt(k.split(',')[0]));
      const allRows = stoneKeys.map(k => parseInt(k.split(',')[1]));
      if (problem.solution?.move) {
        allCols.push(problem.solution.move[0]);
        allRows.push(problem.solution.move[1]);
      }

      // 2-cell padding around the bounding box, clamped to board edges
      const PAD    = 2;
      const minCol = Math.max(0, Math.min(...allCols) - PAD);
      const maxCol = Math.min(boardSize - 1, Math.max(...allCols) + PAD);
      const minRow = Math.max(0, Math.min(...allRows) - PAD);
      const maxRow = Math.min(boardSize - 1, Math.max(...allRows) + PAD);

      const margin = CELL * 0.75;
      let vbX = PADDING + minCol * CELL - margin;
      let vbY = PADDING + minRow * CELL - margin;
      let vbW = (maxCol - minCol) * CELL + margin * 2 + CELL;
      let vbH = (maxRow - minRow) * CELL + margin * 2 + CELL;

      // Force a square viewBox so the SVG fills the square container without
      // letterboxing. A non-square viewBox with height:100%/width:auto (mobile CSS)
      // causes the SVG to be narrower than the container, exposing the container
      // background as a fake "full board" behind the actual cropped SVG.
      const vbSize = Math.max(vbW, vbH);
      if (vbW < vbSize) vbX -= (vbSize - vbW) / 2;
      if (vbH < vbSize) vbY -= (vbSize - vbH) / 2;

      svg.setAttribute('viewBox', `${vbX} ${vbY} ${vbSize} ${vbSize}`);
    }

    // ── Handle user move ─────────────────────────────────────────
    async function onMovePlayed(col, row) {
      if (solved || !currentProblem || isMoving) return;
      isMoving = true;
      attemptNumber++;
      const thisAttempt = attemptNumber; // capture before any await
      updatePip(thisAttempt, 'pending');

      board.setInteractive(false);
      document.getElementById('hint-btn').disabled = true;

      try {
        const res = await API.evaluateMove(
          currentProblem, col, row, thisAttempt, userProfile.current_rank
        );

        if (res.correct) {
          solved = true;
          board.place(col, row, board.getColor());
          board.addMarker(col, row, 'triangle');
          updatePip(thisAttempt, 'correct');
          showCommentary(res.message, 'correct');
          await onSolved();
        } else {
          board.clearJudging(); // remove the judging stone — move was wrong
          updatePip(thisAttempt, 'wrong');
          showCommentary(res.message, 'wrong');

          if (res.solution) {
            showSolution(res.solution);
            await onFailed();
          } else {
            board.setInteractive(true);
          }
        }
      } catch(e) {
        console.error('Evaluate error:', e);
        board.clearJudging();
        showCommentary('Something went wrong. Try again.', '');
        board.setInteractive(true);
      }

      document.getElementById('hint-btn').disabled = false;
      isMoving = false;
    }

    // ── Hint ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    async function getHint() {
      if (!currentProblem || solved) return;
      hintsUsed++;
      const hint = currentProblem.hint || 'Look carefully at the liberties of each group.';
      showCommentary(`💡 ${hint}`, '');
    }

    // ── Solved / Failed ──────────────────────────────────────────
    async function onSolved() {
      board.setInteractive(false);
      document.getElementById('hint-btn').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('skip-btn').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('next-btn').style.display = 'inline-flex';

      totalSolved++;
      document.getElementById('stat-solved').textContent = totalSolved;

      const points = Rank.pointsForProblem(true, hintsUsed, currentProblem.difficulty || 1);
      await updateRankScore(points);
      await Promise.all([
        UserDB.logAttempt(
          userId, currentProblem.id, currentProblem.boardSize || 19,
          currentProblem.topic, true, hintsUsed
        ),
        API.updateSchedule(userId, currentProblem.id, true, hintsUsed, attemptNumber)
          .catch(() => {}), // schedule update is best-effort
      ]);
    }

    async function onFailed() {
      board.setInteractive(false);
      document.getElementById('hint-btn').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('skip-btn').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('next-btn').style.display = 'inline-flex';

      const points = Rank.pointsForProblem(false, 0, currentProblem.difficulty || 1);
      await updateRankScore(points);
      await Promise.all([
        UserDB.logAttempt(
          userId, currentProblem.id, currentProblem.boardSize || 19,
          currentProblem.topic, false, hintsUsed
        ),
        API.updateSchedule(userId, currentProblem.id, false, hintsUsed, attemptNumber)
          .catch(() => {}), // schedule update is best-effort
      ]);
    }

    async function updateRankScore(points) {
      const newScore = Math.max(0, (userProfile.rank_score || 0) + points);
      const newRank  = Rank.scoreToRank(newScore);

      userProfile.rank_score   = newScore;
      userProfile.current_rank = newRank;

      document.getElementById('stat-score').textContent = newScore;
      document.getElementById('stat-rank').textContent  = newRank;

      const wrap = document.getElementById('rank-change-wrap');
      const div  = document.createElement('div');
      div.className   = `rank-change rank-change--${points >= 0 ? 'up' : 'down'}`;
      div.textContent = `${points >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${points} pts`;
      wrap.innerHTML  = '';
      wrap.appendChild(div);

      await UserDB.updateRank(userId, newRank, newScore);
    }

    // ── UI helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    function showCommentary(text, type) {
      const box = document.getElementById('commentary');
      box.textContent = text;
      box.className   = `commentary-box show${type ? ' ' + type : ''}`;
      Glossary.wrap(box);
      // Smoothly bring the result into view — critical on small phones where
      // the board fills much of the screen.
      box.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });
    }

    function showSolution(solution) {
      document.getElementById('solution-text').textContent = solution.explanation;
      document.getElementById('solution-box').className = 'solution-box show';
      if (solution.move) {
        board.addMarker(solution.move[0], solution.move[1], 'flag');
      }
    }

    function resetPips() {
      [1,2,3].forEach(i => {
        document.getElementById(`pip-${i}`).className = 'attempt-pip';
      });
    }

    function updatePip(n, state) {
      const pip = document.getElementById(`pip-${n}`);
      if (!pip) return;
      pip.className = `attempt-pip attempt-pip--${
        state === 'correct' ? 'correct' : state === 'wrong' ? 'wrong' : ''
      }`;
    }

    init();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

  <script>
    async function init() {
      const session = await Auth.getSession();
      if (!session) { location.href = 'auth.html'; return; }

      const userId = session.user.id;
      const profile = await UserDB.getProfile(userId);
      if (!profile) { location.href = 'auth.html'; return; }

      // Hero
      const initial = (profile.display_name || 'S')[0].toUpperCase();
      document.getElementById('avatar-initial').textContent = initial;
      document.getElementById('profile-name').textContent = profile.display_name;
      document.getElementById('profile-rank').textContent = profile.current_rank;

      // Stats
      document.getElementById('stat-score').textContent = profile.rank_score;

      const [{ data: attempts }, { data: games }] = await Promise.all([
        sb.from('problem_attempts').select('id, solved').eq('user_id', userId),
        sb.from('saved_games').select('id').eq('user_id', userId),
      ]);
      const solved = (attempts || []).filter(a => a.solved).length;
      document.getElementById('stat-solved').textContent = solved;
      document.getElementById('stat-games').textContent = (games || []).length;

      // Rank history
      const { data: history } = await sb
        .from('rank_history')
        .select('rank, rank_score, recorded_at')
        .eq('user_id', userId)
        .order('recorded_at', { ascending: false })
        .limit(10);

      const rankList = document.getElementById('rank-list');
      if (history && history.length) {
        rankList.innerHTML = history.map(h => {
          const date = new Date(h.recorded_at).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' });
          return `
            <div class="rank-item">
              <div>
                <p class="rank-item__rank">${h.rank}</p>
                <p class="rank-item__score">${h.rank_score} pts</p>
              </div>
              <span class="rank-item__date">${date}</span>
            </div>
          `;
        }).join('');
      }

      // Account
      document.getElementById('account-email').textContent = session.user.email || '—';
      const since = new Date(profile.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
      document.getElementById('account-since').textContent = since;
    }

    async function signOut() {
      try { await Auth.signOut(); } catch(e) { location.href = 'index.html'; }
    }

    init();
  </script>
</body>

  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@supabase/supabase-js@2.49.4/dist/umd/supabase.min.js"></script>
  <script>
    window.GOTUTOR_CONFIG = {
      supabaseUrl:     'https://humvrkmlvhzwoawoyslu.supabase.co',
      supabaseAnonKey: 'sb_publishable_dEQVVToEQh35MPrBEtKkmQ_om0ty8-r',

  <script>
    const SENSEI_BASE = 'https://senseis.xmp.net/?';
    const MIN_GAMES   = 5;

    // All categories with their unlock thresholds (mirrors _errorCategories.js)
    const ALL_CATEGORIES = {
      capture:    { minScore: 0,    label: 'Capturing & Atari',     sensei: 'Atari' },
      basic_life: { minScore: 0,    label: 'Basic Life & Death',    sensei: 'LifeAndDeath' },
      connection: { minScore: 0,    label: 'Connection & Cutting',  sensei: 'Cutting' },
      ladder:     { minScore: 1000, label: 'Ladders & Nets',        sensei: 'Ladder' },
      ko:         { minScore: 1000, label: 'Ko Fights',             sensei: 'Ko' },
      shape:      { minScore: 1000, label: 'Stone Shape',           sensei: 'Shape' },
      direction:  { minScore: 1000, label: 'Direction of Play',     sensei: 'Direction' },
      joseki:     { minScore: 1500, label: 'Joseki & Opening',      sensei: 'Joseki' },
      influence:  { minScore: 1500, label: 'Influence & Thickness', sensei: 'Thickness' },
      endgame:    { minScore: 2000, label: 'Endgame Precision',     sensei: 'Endgame' },
    };

    let userId      = null;
    let userProfile = null;
    let allGames    = [];

    async function init() {
      const session = await Auth.getSession();
      if (!session) { location.href = 'auth.html'; return; }
      userId      = session.user.id;
      userProfile = await UserDB.getProfile(userId);
      if (!userProfile) { location.href = 'auth.html'; return; }

      document.getElementById('page-sub').textContent =
        `${userProfile.current_rank} · pattern analysis across your games`;

      // Fetch all saved games (we need ai_summary to be present)
      const { data: games } = await sb
        .from('saved_games')
        .select('id, created_at, board_size, source, ai_summary')
        .eq('user_id', userId)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false });

      allGames = games || [];
      hide('loading-state');

      const analyzed   = allGames.filter(g => g.ai_summary !== null && g.ai_summary !== undefined);
      const unanalyzed = allGames.filter(g => g.ai_summary === null || g.ai_summary === undefined);

      // Show backfill option if there are unanalyzed games
      if (unanalyzed.length > 0) {
        if (analyzed.length < MIN_GAMES) {
          show('gate-section');
          show('backfill-card');
          const pct = Math.round((analyzed.length / MIN_GAMES) * 100);
          document.getElementById('gate-bar').style.width = pct + '%';
          document.getElementById('gate-bar-label').textContent =
            `${analyzed.length} of ${MIN_GAMES} games analyzed`;
          document.getElementById('gate-sub').textContent =
            `Analyze at least ${MIN_GAMES} games to unlock pattern analysis.` +
            (unanalyzed.length > 0 ? ` You have ${unanalyzed.length} game${unanalyzed.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''} ready to analyze.` : '');
        } else {
          show('backfill-card-inline');
          document.getElementById('backfill-card-inline').querySelector('.backfill-card__sub').textContent =
            `${unanalyzed.length} saved game${unanalyzed.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''} haven't been analyzed yet. Analyze them to improve pattern accuracy.`;
          await runAnalysis(analyzed);
        }
      } else if (analyzed.length < MIN_GAMES) {
        show('gate-section');
        const pct = Math.round((analyzed.length / MIN_GAMES) * 100);
        document.getElementById('gate-bar').style.width = pct + '%';
        document.getElementById('gate-bar-label').textContent =
          `${analyzed.length} of ${MIN_GAMES} games analyzed`;
        document.getElementById('gate-sub').textContent =
          `Play and review at least ${MIN_GAMES} games to unlock pattern analysis.`;
      } else {
        await runAnalysis(analyzed);
      }
    }

    async function runAnalysis(analyzedGames) {
      const games = analyzedGames || allGames.filter(g => g.ai_summary !== null && g.ai_summary !== undefined);

      hide('error-state');
      show('analysis-section');
      document.getElementById('analysis-section').innerHTML =
        '<div class="loading-state"><span class="loader"></span><span>Analyzing patterns…</span></div>';

      try {
        const res = await fetch('/.netlify/functions/analyze-patterns', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            games: games.map(g => ({ id: g.id, ai_summary: g.ai_summary, created_at: g.created_at })),
            rank: userProfile.current_rank,
            rankScore: userProfile.rank_score || 0,
          }),
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Analysis failed');

        if (data.insufficient) {
          document.getElementById('analysis-section').innerHTML = '';
          hide('analysis-section');
          show('gate-section');
          const pct = Math.round((data.gamesAnalyzed / MIN_GAMES) * 100);
          document.getElementById('gate-bar').style.width = pct + '%';
          document.getElementById('gate-bar-label').textContent =
            `${data.gamesAnalyzed} of ${MIN_GAMES} games analyzed`;
          return;
        }

        renderAnalysis(data, games.length);
      } catch (e) {
        hide('analysis-section');
        document.getElementById('error-msg').textContent = e.message || 'Analysis failed.';
        show('error-state');
      }
    }

    function renderAnalysis(data, gamesAnalyzed) {
      // Rebuild analysis section from scratch
      const sec = document.getElementById('analysis-section');
      sec.innerHTML = '';
      sec.style.display = '';

      // Progress notes
      if (data.progressNotes) {
        const notes = document.createElement('div');
        notes.className = 'notes-card';
        notes.style.animationDelay = '0s';
        notes.textContent = data.progressNotes;
        sec.appendChild(notes);
      }

      // Weak areas
      if (data.topWeakAreas?.length) {
        const maxCount = data.topWeakAreas[0].count || 1;

        const label = document.createElement('p');
        label.className = 'section-label';
        label.style.marginTop = '1.5rem';
        label.textContent = 'Recurring Weak Areas';
        sec.appendChild(label);

        const list = document.createElement('div');
        list.className = 'weak-areas';
        data.topWeakAreas.forEach((area, i) => {
          const pct   = Math.round((area.count / maxCount) * 100);
          const meta  = ALL_CATEGORIES[area.category] || {};
          const delay = (i * 0.05).toFixed(2);
          list.innerHTML += `
            <div class="weak-card" style="animation-delay:${delay}s">
              <div class="weak-card__header">
                <span class="weak-card__label">${area.label || meta.label || area.category}</span>
                <span class="weak-card__count">${area.count}×</span>
              </div>
              <div class="weak-card__bar-wrap">
                <div class="weak-card__bar-fill" style="width:${pct}%"></div>
              </div>
              <p class="weak-card__explanation">${area.explanation || ''}</p>
              ${meta.sensei ? `<a class="weak-card__link" href="${SENSEI_BASE}${meta.sensei}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Read about this on Sensei's Library →</a>` : ''}
            </div>
          `;
        });
        sec.appendChild(list);
      }

      // Study priorities
      if (data.studyPriorities?.length) {
        const label = document.createElement('p');
        label.className = 'section-label';
        label.style.marginTop = '1.5rem';
        label.textContent = 'Study Priorities';
        sec.appendChild(label);

        const list = document.createElement('div');
        list.className = 'priorities-list';
        data.studyPriorities.forEach((p, i) => {
          const delay = (i * 0.05).toFixed(2);
          const url   = p.senseiKeyword ? `${SENSEI_BASE}${p.senseiKeyword}` : null;
          list.innerHTML += `
            <div class="priority-item" style="animation-delay:${delay}s">
              <span class="priority-num">${p.priority || i + 1}</span>
              <div>
                <p class="priority-item__topic">${p.topic}</p>
                <p class="priority-item__reason">${p.reason || ''}</p>
                ${url ? `<a class="priority-item__link" href="${url}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Sensei's Library →</a>` : ''}
              </div>
            </div>
          `;
        });
        sec.appendChild(list);
      }

      // Wrap Go terms in Claude-generated content
      Glossary.wrap(sec);

      // Meta
      const meta = document.createElement('p');
      meta.className = 'analysis-meta';
      meta.style.marginTop = '1rem';
      meta.textContent = `Based on ${gamesAnalyzed} analyzed game${gamesAnalyzed !== 1 ? 's' : ''} · ${userProfile.current_rank}`;
      sec.appendChild(meta);

      // Refresh button
      const btn = document.createElement('button');
      btn.className = 'btn btn--secondary';
      btn.style.cssText = 'width:100%;margin:0.75rem 0';
      btn.textContent = 'Refresh analysis';
      btn.onclick = () => runAnalysis();
      sec.appendChild(btn);

      // Locked categories
      const rankScore  = userProfile.rank_score || 0;
      const locked = Object.entries(ALL_CATEGORIES)
        .filter(([, meta]) => rankScore < meta.minScore);
      if (locked.length) {
        const lockLabel = document.createElement('p');
        lockLabel.className = 'section-label';
        lockLabel.style.marginTop = '1.25rem';
        lockLabel.textContent = 'Unlocks as you improve';
        sec.appendChild(lockLabel);

        const grid = document.createElement('div');
        grid.className = 'locked-grid';
        locked.forEach(([, meta]) => {
          grid.innerHTML += `<span class="locked-chip"><span class="locked-chip__lock">🔒</span>${meta.label}</span>`;
        });
        sec.appendChild(grid);
      }
    }

    // ── Backfill: loop through unanalyzed games one at a time ────────────────
    let backfillRunning = false;

    async function startBackfill() {
      if (backfillRunning) return;
      backfillRunning = true;

      const btnGate   = document.getElementById('backfill-btn');
      const btnInline = document.getElementById('backfill-btn-inline');
      const progGate  = document.getElementById('backfill-progress');
      const progInline= document.getElementById('backfill-progress-inline');

      if (btnGate)   btnGate.disabled   = true;
      if (btnInline) btnInline.disabled = true;

      const unanalyzed = allGames.filter(g => g.ai_summary === null || g.ai_summary === undefined);
      let processed = 0;

      const setProgress = msg => {
        if (progGate)   progGate.textContent   = msg;
        if (progInline) progInline.textContent = msg;
      };

      for (const game of unanalyzed) {
        setProgress(`Analyzing game ${processed + 1} of ${unanalyzed.length}…`);
        try {
          // Re-fetch the full SGF for this game
          const { data: full } = await sb
            .from('saved_games')
            .select('sgf_content, board_size')
            .eq('id', game.id)
            .single();

          if (!full?.sgf_content) { processed++; continue; }

          const rank        = userProfile.current_rank || '20 kyu';
          const playerColor = 'B';

          const res = await fetch('/.netlify/functions/game-summary', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              sgf: full.sgf_content,
              boardSize: full.board_size,
              rank,
              playerColor,
            }),
          });

          if (res.ok) {
            const { summary } = await res.json();
            if (summary) {
              // Save ai_summary back to the saved_games row
              await sb.from('saved_games').update({ ai_summary: summary }).eq('id', game.id);
              // Update local cache
              const idx = allGames.findIndex(g => g.id === game.id);
              if (idx !== -1) allGames[idx].ai_summary = summary;
            }
          }
        } catch (e) {
          console.warn('Backfill error for game', game.id, e.message);
        }
        processed++;
      }

      setProgress(`Done — analyzed ${processed} game${processed !== 1 ? 's' : ''}.`);
      backfillRunning = false;

      // Re-run pattern analysis with the newly analyzed games
      const analyzed = allGames.filter(g => g.ai_summary !== null && g.ai_summary !== undefined);
      hide('gate-section');
      hide('backfill-card-inline');
      if (analyzed.length >= MIN_GAMES) {
        await runAnalysis(analyzed);
      } else {
        show('gate-section');
        const pct = Math.round((analyzed.length / MIN_GAMES) * 100);
        document.getElementById('gate-bar').style.width = pct + '%';
        document.getElementById('gate-bar-label').textContent =
          `${analyzed.length} of ${MIN_GAMES} games analyzed`;
      }
    }

    function show(id) { const el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.style.display = ''; }
    function hide(id) { const el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.style.display = 'none'; }

    init();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

    window.GOTUTOR_CONFIG = {
      supabaseUrl:     'https://humvrkmlvhzwoawoyslu.supabase.co',
